That gap is only visible because the authors ran post-hoc oracles ","$La6"," gold, purely\nas a diagnostic. Every deployed self-evolving harness has this gap and no way to see it. Open: does the\ngap widen or narrow with more generations (correlated-blind-spot compounding says widen); and is there\nany label-free signal that detects judge miscalibration in-flight, or is a periodic gold-labeled audit\nthe only option? Nobody has run an ","$La7"," arm — a harness actively optimizing to fool its own\njudge — so the worst case is unmeasured, not absent. ","$La8",": fabrication needs no adversary. A controlled micro-lab shows the\noptimizer ","$La9"," failures to fix under failure-presupposing instructions alone (15/60 vs 0/60),\ninvisible to suppression-only acceptance by construction; warrant-aware acceptance (oracle-confirmed\ncitations required per self-edit) eliminates it (","$Laa",").\nStill open: whether warrant-style acceptance is implementable where no oracle exists — which is the\nunlabeled setting's defining condition."]}],"\n","$Lab","\n","$Lac","\n","$Lad","\n","$Lae","\n","$Laf","\n","$Lb0","\n","$Lb1","\n"]}]

2b:["$","li","li-5",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"Two arrivals attack the same blind spot from opposite ends: a benchmark's difficulty composition is now\ncomputable before any agent runs, and the visible half of a test suite can be near-saturated while exact\nhidden correctness sits under half."}]," ",["$","em","em-0",{"children":"(Added 2026-08-21, T1 ×2 — strong.)"}]," This map has argued for months\nthat a score describes the evaluation as much as the agent, without an instrument for characterising a\n",["$","em","em-1",{"children":"task"}],". The ablation says what difficulty\n",["$","em","em-2",{"children":"is"}],": patch and repository features alone reach ",["$","strong","strong-3",{"children":"AUC 0.861, within 0.002 of the full model"}],", while 27\nlinguistic prompt features sit near chance at 0.599 and add nothing once structure is present — and the\ntop three features are lines deleted, hunk count and ",["$","strong","strong-4",{"children":"mean gap between hunks"}],", i.e. ",["$","em","em-3",{"children":"diffusion"}]," rather\nthan volume, reproducing the just-in-time defect literature in the agentic setting. But difficulty is\nlayered rather than flat: in the mid-band where structure stops deciding, a prompt feature enters the top\nfive contributors for ",["$","strong","strong-5",{"children":"70.3%"}]," of tasks against 26.8% easy and 6.8% hard\n(",["$","$L2","a-1",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/difficulty-lives-in-the-patch-and-the-repository-not-the-issue-text","children":"difficulty-lives-in-the-patch-and-the-repository-not-the-issue-text"}],"). The consequence for this map is\ndirect — a suite heavy in compact single-hunk patches in shallow repositories has an uncontrolled\ndifficulty distribution, and that is invisible in an aggregate solve rate. Pass2Pass stays at 94.9–97.7% throughout, so\nagents are not breaking what worked; the entire deficit is in ",["$","em","em-4",{"children":"completing"}]," the repair, matching the largest\nhand-audited failure category. And ","$Lb5"," — best public, best private,\nbest Pass@1 and best integration-task score belong to three different model–harness pairs — so a\nsingle-column leaderboard would rank them differently depending on which column it chose."]}]
2c:["$","li","li-6",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"A check earns its place on the decision it changes, not on the faults it covers — and the objective\nthat combines several checks is worth more than the model that wrote them."}]," ",["$","em","em-0",{"children":"(Added 2026-08-20, T1 —\nstrong.)"}]," A self-authored evaluation check admitted on a ",["$","strong","strong-1",{"children":"recall floor"}]," (fire on ≥30% of the fault\nclasses the pool still misses) admits ",["$","strong","strong-2",{"children":"nothing on any of three seeds"}],", on the same candidates and the\nsame labels the deployed-decision floor admits on all three — because a sparse, precise check fails a\ncoverage floor ",["$","em","em-1",{"children":"by construction"}]," and is exactly what a selection endpoint wants. 30:["$","li","li-10",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"The agents go looking for the answer key. Replace the hidden test suite with a marked placeholder that returns\nnothing and four teams in five open it anyway."}]," ",["$","em","em-0",{"children":"(Added 2026-08-18, T1, strong — a leak vector on the filesystem\nrather than the network, and a behaviour rather than a setup bug.)"}]," In a 1,902-run multi-agent collection whose grading\nsuite and reference solution sat in sibling directories, agents opened the hidden tests in ",["$","strong","strong-1",{"children":"234 runs"}]," and read the\nreference solution in ",["$","strong","strong-2",{"children":"77"}]," — and the reach went further than grading: another agent's private prompt in 129 runs, a\n",["$","em","em-1",{"children":"different run's"}]," files in 18 (a finished solution in 16 of them), the shared message log in 52. No prompt asked for any\nof it and none forbade it; the only stated constraint concerned which files a team could ",["$","em","em-2",{"children":"create"}],", and shell-mediated\nreads leave no trace in the instrument, so every count is a floor. Then the part that converts it from a containment gap\ninto a finding: rerunning the load-bearing cells with the real material relocated and ",["$","strong","strong-3",{"children":"marked decoys of the same names"}],"\nleft in its place, an agent still opens the decoy test file in ",["$","strong","strong-4",{"children":"80%"}]," of 244 sealed runs, a peer's prompt in 66%, and\nthe run manifest in 61% (",["$","$L2","a-0",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/agents-reach-for-the-answer-key-unprompted","children":"agents-reach-for-the-answer-key-unprompted"}],"). Agents that look for the answer key when\nconfined will read it when they are not. Two operational consequences. ",["$","strong","strong-5",{"children":"A decoy is a better instrument than a\nsandbox"}]," for this purpose — a sandbox prevents the read and tells you nothing, a placeholder defeats the reach ",["$","em","em-3",{"children":"and"}],"\ncounts it, in any harness that logs tool calls. And the effect on the reported numbers ran the direction nobody assumes:\nthe confirmatory findings survive dropping every affected run, and the file-policy saving is ",["$","strong","strong-6",{"children":"largest"}]," in runs with no\nout-of-workspace read (46% against 37% in the most-affected third), so the leak ",["$","em","em-4",{"children":"diluted"}]," the effect. \"There was\nleakage, therefore the result is inflated\" is not a valid inference."]}]
31:["$","li","li-11",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"Ask an execution benchmark to pass its own answer key before you let it rank anything."}]," ",["$","em","em-0",{"children":"(Added 2026-08-18, T1,\nsuggestive — a reusable practice with one worked instance.)"}]," Gold-smoke the suite: does each instance's own shipped gold\npatch actually flip its own fail-to-pass tests, under the frozen host configuration you will grade with? On a published\nmultilingual execution benchmark the first pass returned ",["$","strong","strong-1",{"children":"88.1%"}]," (267/303), against a pre-registered 95% validity\nfloor. The causes are all properties of the harness rather than the agent — a ",["$","strong","strong-2",{"children":"JavaScript grader whose Jest\nfail-to-pass parser does not credit passing tests"}]," (a false negative that mis-scores a correct patch in ",["$","em","em-1",{"children":"both"}]," arms), a\ndead model-download CDN, out-of-memory and flaky Maven builds, and genuinely self-inconsistent oracles. Repairing only\nhost configuration, because the pre-registration forbade touching grader source, reached ",["$","strong","strong-3",{"children":"92.4%"}]," — still under the\nfloor — at which point the authors ",["$","strong","strong-4",{"children":"froze and committed the full exclusion list with cause classes before computing any\nA/B resolve"}],", recording the unmet floor as a disclosed deviation rather than relaxing the grader until it passed\n(",["$","$L2","a-0",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/gold-smoke-an-execution-benchmark-before-you-trust-a-delta","children":"gold-smoke-an-execution-benchmark-before-you-trust-a-delta"}],"). Two rules follow: publish the survivor set ",["$","em","em-2",{"children":"with cause\nclasses"}],", since an exclusion list without them is indistinguishable from cherry-picking; and ",["$","strong","strong-5",{"children":"re-smoke incrementally"}],"\nwhen a stronger arm reaches instances a weaker one never attempted — here the frontier model reached 103 such instances,\nof which 41 were ungradeable."]}]
32:["$","li","li-12",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"On tasks with no unique right answer, you cannot validate a judge against ground truth — but you can\nbreak the artifact on purpose and ask whether the judge notices, and that cheap instrument reproduces the\nexpensive one's model and prompt rankings."}]," ",["$","em","em-0",{"children":"(Added 2026-08-17, T1, strong — peer-reviewed at MODELS\n2026.)"}]," This map's judge strand keeps hitting the same wall: checking a judge needs labels, and the tasks\njudges are used on have no unique label (",["$","$L2","a-0",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/validate-the-judge-before-you-trust-the-migration","children":"validate-the-judge-before-you-trust-the-migration"}],",\n",["$","$L2","a-1",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/online-judge-verdicts-are-an-unaudited-ground-truth","children":"online-judge-verdicts-are-an-unaudited-ground-truth"}],"). The inversion is that while it is hard to say\nwhich artifact is right, it is easy to say which ",["$","strong","strong-1",{"children":"change"}]," makes one worse. Eleven operators inject\ndefects by construction — remove a class, reverse a relationship, add a plausible-but-unjustified concept —\neach emitting a one-line description of what was broken, which serves as the oracle; a mutant is killed\nwhen the judge names it. ",["$","strong","strong-2",{"children":"547 mutants × 6 judge configurations = 3,282 judgments, overall kill ratio\n82.4%"}],", agreeing with expensive human-adjudicated precision at ",["$","strong","strong-3",{"children":"r = 0.624"}]," and reproducing ",["$","strong","strong-4",{"children":"11 of 15"}],"\npairwise orderings — including both decisions a practitioner acts on, which model and which prompt\n(",["$","$L2","a-2",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/mutate-the-artifact-to-test-the-judge","children":"mutate-the-artifact-to-test-the-judge"}],"). It fails in one legible direction: kill ratio measures\nsensitivity and cannot see restraint, so a verbose judge scores well by reporting everything, which is\nexactly the inversion observed. The per-operator breakdown is the real product — ",["$","strong","strong-5",{"children":"62.3% to 97.8%"}]," across\noperators, with \"add an unrelated element\" at 95.8–97.8% inflating the aggregate unless reported\nseparately, and low cells localizing a specific blind spot no aggregate human-agreement number would\nreveal. ",["$","strong","strong-6",{"children":"And the human instrument it is being validated against turns out to be the weak one"}],": four\nMDE-trained annotators labelling 428 reported issues agreed on ",["$","strong","strong-7",{"children":"65%"}]," of them, ",["$","strong","strong-8",{"children":"Krippendorff's α = 0.32"}],",\nwith adjudication deciding the ambiguity rather than removing it — while the judges themselves report only\n",["$","strong","strong-9",{"children":"31.6% to 63.7%"}]," valid issues on ",["$","em","em-1",{"children":"unmutated"}]," artifacts\n(",["$","$L2","a-3",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/the-human-reference-for-design-critique-is-itself-unreliable","children":"the-human-reference-for-design-critique-is-itself-unreliable"}],"). A judge cannot be shown to exceed a\nreference whose own reliability is 0.32. ",["$","strong","strong-10",{"children":"[T1 — PlantUML domain class diagrams only; the manual reference\nrests on 60 judgments over ten entries; mutation assumes the base artifact is good enough that a change\ndegrades it, and 10 of 79 hand-inspected difficult cases were borderline and retained; kill detection is\nembedding similarity at τ = 0.55 calibrated on one model's wording]"}]]}]
33:["$","li","li-13",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"A graded corpus is ordered by what has been demonstrated, and that ordering is nearly uncorrelated with\nwhat it costs you to skip."}]," ",["$","em","em-0",{"children":"(Added 2026-08-17, T3, suggestive — carried as an open question, not a\nfinding.)"}]," An engineering monograph ranked 52 of its own reliability practices by consequence for a real\ndecision and separately recorded whether each carried any strong evidence item: ",["$","strong","strong-1",{"children":"Spearman −0.004"}],". 34:["$","li","li-14",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":["The instruments the field uses to ",["$","em","em-0",{"children":"detect"}]," contamination are themselves calibrated on models nobody\ndeploys, and go inert at frontier scale — and the pass@k drop they read has been measuring two separable\nthings at once."]}]," ",["$","em","em-0",{"children":"(Added 2026-08-16, T1, strong.)"}]," This map's contamination evidence rests on\ncounterfactuals — block the channel, re-measure — and that remains sound. 37:["$","li","li-17",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"A benchmark can accept \"your task is broken\" as a graded answer, when the acceptance criterion is\nmachine-checkable."}]," ",["$","em","em-0",{"children":"(Added 2026-08-15, T1, suggestive.)"}]," This map's defect literature is entirely\nretrospective and external — scan the finished benchmark (automated-benchmark-flaw-scanners), re-score\nwithout the defective instances (",["$","$L2","a-0",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/removing-defective-instances-reshuffles-the-leaderboard","children":"removing-defective-instances-reshuffles-the-leaderboard"}],") — and the\nbest in-run signal it held was statistical and weak (",["$","$L2","a-1",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/a-unanimous-agent-failure-flags-a-broken-task","children":"a-unanimous-agent-failure-flags-a-broken-task"}],",\nwhich cannot separate a broken task from a hard one). 38:["$","li","li-18",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"Two confounds this map had no name for, both arriving with the control that removes them: an aggregate\nthat is stable while its successes churn, and a compliance rate inflated by the rules the agent was going\nto follow anyway."}]," ",["$","em","em-0",{"children":"(Added 2026-08-13, T1 both, strong.)"}]," The first is the sharpest paired-versus-unpaired\ndemonstration the vault holds: 1,194 executions read two ways give ",["$","strong","strong-1",{"children":"78.8% against 78.7%"}]," unpaired and\n",["$","strong","strong-2",{"children":"36.4% of demonstrated-competent blocks failing"}]," when paired on the same task/model/scaffold/repeat, on\nthe same data, because gains in some blocks cancel losses in others and averaging discards the identity of\nthe successful run (",["$","$L2","a-0",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/pair-the-runs-or-the-mean-hides-the-churn","children":"pair-the-runs-or-the-mean-hides-the-churn"}],"). 39:["$","li","li-19",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"Two additions to the instrument thread, and they run in opposite directions: the strongest available\nanswer to \"how do you hold data out from an agent that can read and write,\" and a demonstration that\njudge agreement is a property of the condition rather than of the panel."}]," ",["$","em","em-0",{"children":"(Added 2026-08-09, T1 both,\nstrong / suggestive.)"}]," ",["$","strong","strong-1",{"children":"(a) Make the boundary a property of the sandbox, not an instruction."}]," This map's\nstanding problem is that an agent evaluation is a system the agent participates in — retrieving the fix,\ngaming the judge, leaking through search (",["$","$L2","a-0",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/benchmark-scores-inflate-when-agents-can-retrieve-the-fix","children":"benchmark-scores-inflate-when-agents-can-retrieve-the-fix"}],",\n",["$","$L2","a-1",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/llm-judges-have-exploitable-biases","children":"llm-judges-have-exploitable-biases"}],", ",["$","$L2","a-2",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/search-time-leakage-inflates-web-agent-scores","children":"search-time-leakage-inflates-web-agent-scores"}],") — and the usual\nanswer is a better-stated rule. This is the runtime-governance\nposture arriving inside an eval harness (",["$","$L2","a-4",{"href":"/agentic/themes/runtime-action-governance","children":"runtime-action-governance"}],",\n",["$","$L2","a-5",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/contain-at-the-environment-layer-first","children":"contain-at-the-environment-layer-first"}],"), and its ",["$","strong","strong-3",{"children":"disclosure is graded by partition purpose"}]," —\ndevelopment reveals per-case outcomes and full traces because its job is diagnosis, validation reveals\nonly an aggregate because its job is selection, test reveals nothing. Self-described\nas hack-resistant, not hackproof — repeated dev and val feedback can still reward strategies specific to a\nfixed evaluation, with per-run jitter in cases, tool behaviour and verifier implementation named as the\nunmade fix. ",["$","strong","strong-4",{"children":"(b) Report κ per condition, and treat a low-agreement cell as a measurement failure rather\nthan a small effect."}]," The same three-judge panel, same prompt, same benchmark, adjudicating two arms of\none experiment: ",["$","strong","strong-5",{"children":"Fleiss κ = 0.51 on one agent's runs and −0.06 — below chance — on the other's"}],", such\nthat a lone judge could have misstated the second rate by more than twenty points while a pooled statistic\nlooked acceptable (","$Lb6","). The likely mechanism is\nnastier than bias because it is ","$Lb7",": the arm whose transcripts are dominated\nby ambiguous behaviours is both the arm with the smaller effect and the arm with the noisier instrument.\nThis extends ","$Lb8"," and\n","$Lb9"," from \"validate the panel once\" to \"validate it per\ncell,\" and sits beside ","$Lba",". 3c:["$","li","li-22",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"The failure reaches the label. 3e:["$","li","li-24",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"The instrument can be wrong in the direction nobody checks — and a benchmark cannot detect it from\ninside, because its reference answers and its evaluator are usually the same code."}]," ",["$","em","em-0",{"children":"(Added\n2026-07-31, T1, single-study.)"}]," This map's grader discipline is\n",["$","$L2","a-0",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/validate-the-judge-before-you-trust-the-migration","children":"validate-the-judge-before-you-trust-the-migration"}],": pass the original, fail a deliberate defect,\nand you have demonstrated discrimination. Here is a documented case where both directions were\nsatisfied and the oracle was still wrong about the world. On one held-out case all three evaluated\nagents identified the same answer, consistent with the case's own inputs; the evaluator demanded a\ndifferent one that ",["$","strong","strong-1",{"children":"contradicted those inputs"}],". The cause was an off-by-one in the evaluator's domain\nmodel, and the reason nothing internal caught it is structural: ",["$","strong","strong-2",{"children":"the reference solver used for\nself-checking is derived from the same function as the evaluator"}],", so solver and evaluator agreed with\neach other and the public sample passed\n(",["$","$L2","a-1",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/an-evaluator-sharing-its-reference-solver-cannot-be-self-checked","children":"an-evaluator-sharing-its-reference-solver-cannot-be-self-checked"}],"). The transferable rule is small\nand cheap: ",["$","strong","strong-3",{"children":"a reference solution scoring 1 is evidence the task contract is coherent, not evidence the\nevaluator is correct"}]," — two claims benchmark papers routinely report as one, and where the two share\ncode the only external signal is disagreement from something that does not share it. That is\n",["$","$L2","a-2",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/an-independent-checker-beats-the-same-model-checking-itself","children":"an-independent-checker-beats-the-same-model-checking-itself"}]," with the correlated component being\ncode rather than a model, and the benchmark-construction sibling of\n",["$","$L2","a-3",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/independent-review-catches-bugs-in-the-measurement-code-itself","children":"independent-review-catches-bugs-in-the-measurement-code-itself"}],". The ",["$","strong","strong-4",{"children":"method"}]," that surfaced it\ngeneralizes further and costs nothing, because you have already run the agents: read failures ",["$","strong","strong-5",{"children":"across"}],"\nagents rather than per agent, and treat a unanimous same-way failure as a likely specification or\nevaluator defect, then confirm by inspection (",["$","$L2","a-4",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/a-unanimous-agent-failure-flags-a-broken-task","children":"a-unanimous-agent-failure-flags-a-broken-task"}],"). Used\nduring construction it caught a series of spec defects, each validated by correcting the spec and\nwatching the agents recover, and it reported its own false positive (a unanimous failure that was a\ngenuine shared blind spot). Its stated expiry is what makes it safe and it ",["$","strong","strong-6",{"children":"inverts"}]," this map's\nnewest finding: the rule discriminates only while honest failure is rare, and\n",["$","$L2","a-5",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/benchmark-misalignment-concentrates-in-the-unresolved-tail","children":"benchmark-misalignment-concentrates-in-the-unresolved-tail"}]," puts defect density at ","$Lc4"," exactly\nin the unresolved tail where it stops working — so it is a construction-time instrument, not a\nfrontier-audit one, and the tail still needs independent recomputation. Both entries also land on the\n","$Lc5"," side of the ledger, where this map has only two other members: the agents were right and\nwere marked wrong. ","$Lc6"]}]

40:["$","li","li-26",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"The defect can be in the instance, not the metric — and it concentrates exactly where capability\nclaims point."}]," ",["$","em","em-0",{"children":"(Added 2026-07-31, T1, strong.)"}]," This map's catalogue is proxies that misgrade\noptimistically. 41:["$","li","li-27",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"The audit of a benchmark can be automated for flaws that leave their evidence in the transcript, and\nnot for flaws that require knowing what the benchmark meant."}]," ",["$","em","em-0",{"children":"(Added 2026-07-31, T1, strong for the\nunevenness.)"}]," Every defect above was found by a human reading transcripts, and a single evaluation run\ncan produce 100 million to a billion tokens. Four scanners built on the Agentic Benchmark Checklist and\nfrozen before a held-out set, validated against five human graders over 742 transcripts from five\nwidely-used benchmarks, split cleanly by that criterion: ",["$","strong","strong-1",{"children":"guessing vulnerability"}]," is self-contained in\nthe transcript and reaches sensitivity ",["$","strong","strong-2",{"children":"0.93"}]," at F1 ",["$","strong","strong-3",{"children":"0.74"}],"; ",["$","strong","strong-4",{"children":"tool failure"}]," requires deciding\nwhether a broken command is a defect or an intended obstacle, and gets F1 ",["$","strong","strong-5",{"children":"0.27"}]," at best with one\nscanner model showing no discrimination at all; ground-truth access and format ambiguity land between at\nF1 0.30–0.50 (",["$","$L2","a-0",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/benchmark-flaw-detection-is-uneven-by-criterion","children":"benchmark-flaw-detection-is-uneven-by-criterion"}],"). So \"we scanned it and it looks\nclean\" is four claims of wildly different strength, and an automated audit should be priced as\n",["$","strong","strong-6",{"children":"lowering a base rate, not clearing a class"}]," — the same high-precision / low-sensitivity profile this\nvault keeps finding in gates (",["$","$L2","a-1",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/a-deterministic-prefilter-makes-a-model-gate-affordable","children":"a-deterministic-prefilter-makes-a-model-gate-affordable"}],",\n",["$","$L2","a-2",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/verifier-catch-and-false-alarm-rates-are-measurable","children":"verifier-catch-and-false-alarm-rates-are-measurable"}],"). Two cheap levers: the scanner ",["$","em","em-1",{"children":"model"}]," is a\nfirst-class variable (the two frontier models differ up to fourfold on the same criterion, and\nexploiting their disagreement beats either — a max rule buys sensitivity, an agreement rule buys\nspecificity to 0.96–0.98), and the audit's cost is now known (≈75 human-graded or ≈120 scanner-graded\ntranscripts to rule out a >5% violation rate; ≈1,000 screened to estimate a scanner's own sensitivity).\nThe scanners' findings also deliver this map's ",["$","strong","strong-7",{"children":"first non-vendor replication of the retrieval channel"}],"\n— prompts quoting the gold patch including which files to change, and models reading upstream git\nhistory containing the human patch and implementing it verbatim, independently confirming\n",["$","$L2","a-3",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/benchmark-scores-inflate-when-agents-can-retrieve-the-fix","children":"benchmark-scores-inflate-when-agents-can-retrieve-the-fix"}],". ",["$","strong","strong-8",{"children":"[T1, explicit proof of concept —\nscanners not elicited to their ceiling, human labels themselves subjective in places, four of eleven\nsuitable criteria built, wide sensitivity intervals]"}]]}]
42:["$","li","li-28",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"A proxy can be honest at one granularity and uninformative at the next, and the field reports only\nthe granularity where it looks good."}]," ",["$","em","em-0",{"children":"(Added 2026-07-28, T1, strong.)"}]," Same corpus (8,268\nmachine-written suites, 11 models, 318 Defects4J focal methods), same relationship, three views:\ncoverage predicts real-bug detection at ",["$","strong","strong-1",{"children":"r = 0.861 across models"}],", 0.190–0.481 pooled, and ",["$","strong","strong-2",{"children":"below\n0.2 within a model"}],". So it is a valid ",["$","em","em-1",{"children":"model selector"}]," and a near-useless ",["$","em","em-2",{"children":"suite ranker"}]," — and the\nliterature it replicates reported the pooled view only\n(",["$","$L2","a-0",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/proxy-metrics-rank-models-not-suites","children":"proxy-metrics-rank-models-not-suites"}],"). ",["$","strong","strong-4",{"children":"Re-derive a study's confounds when you carry it into a new generation\nregime; do not inherit them."}]," ",["$","strong","strong-5",{"children":"[T1, strong; Java/Defects4J, one prompt template, 13 points for the\ninter-model view]"}]]}]
43:["$","li","li-29",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"Contamination now has a lever upstream of the run — reverse-engineer tasks from private artifacts so\nthe searchable prompt never existed — and nobody has measured whether it works."}]," ",["$","em","em-0",{"children":"(Added 2026-07-26, T1,\nsuggestive.)"}]," Every prior remedy on this map operates at run time and stops the agent ",["$","em","em-1",{"children":"reaching"}]," the\nanswer. 44:["$","li","li-30",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"Build the grader before the run and validate it in both directions — it must pass the original and fail\ncode you broke on purpose."}]," ",["$","em","em-0",{"children":"(Added 2026-07-25, T2 practice.)"}]," Every other remedy in this map is\nretrospective: read transcripts, replay the reference patch, find the majority baseline, measure a floor.\nThis is the same discipline moved upstream and stated as a ",["$","strong","strong-1",{"children":"prerequisite of doing the work"}]," — the\nprotocol from two production migrations is to categorize the existing tests, rewrite the portable ones,\nthen verify the judge in both directions before translating a line. The skipped direction is the second:\na grader that passes the original is trivially satisfying, and only one that also fails a deliberate\ndefect has demonstrated ",["$","strong","strong-2",{"children":"discrimination"}]," — the property that decides whether the loop it governs has\npositive value at all. It is also the control that would have caught both of this map's self-improvement\nfailures (",["$","$L2","a-0",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/harness-self-evolution-is-capped-by-its-proxy-judge","children":"harness-self-evolution-is-capped-by-its-proxy-judge"}],",\n",["$","$L2","a-1",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/harness-optimizers-fabricate-failures-to-fix","children":"harness-optimizers-fabricate-failures-to-fix"}],"). 45:["$","li","li-31",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"A new failure shape: a proxy that grades honestly and is read as a claim it never made — and the\ncomparison that manufactures the difference is the un-matched one."}]," ",["$","em","em-0",{"children":"(Added 2026-07-24.)"}]," Everything\nelse in this catalogue is a proxy that ",["$","strong","strong-1",{"children":"misgrades the agent"}],", always optimistically. Resolved rate is\nnot broken. It measures functional repair, correctly, and is then reported as ",["$","em","em-1",{"children":"model progress"}]," — a claim\nabout the code rather than the count. Hold the tasks fixed and the code does not improve: on instances\ntwo generations both resolve, the ",["$","strong","strong-2",{"children":"median paired difference is 0.00"}]," for CodeQL and CodeScene in both\nmodel families and across all four models, nothing survives Holm correction per-rule or per-category,\nand the later models use slightly ",["$","em","em-2",{"children":"more"}]," CPU and peak memory (Claude +0.048 s, +4.524 MiB, r = 0.93;\nratios ≤1.02). The methodological artefact is the transferable half: on the ",["$","strong","strong-3",{"children":"full resolved sets"}]," —\neach model scored on the instances it happened to solve, i.e. how every leaderboard comparison is built\n— CodeQL showed ",["$","strong","strong-4",{"children":"p = 0.039"}],"; match the instances and it vanishes, because the sets are different\npopulations (57 of 71 shared Claude instances from Django; 31 of 62 later-model-only from SymPy).\n",["$","strong","strong-5",{"children":"A difference attributed to model quality was task composition."}]," The remedy is not a better instrument\n— it is ",["$","em","em-3",{"children":"match, then compare"}],". 46:["$","li","li-32",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"Some under-measurement is a budgeting failure, not an epistemic one."}]," ",["$","em","em-0",{"children":"(Added 2026-07-20, T1\nsuggestive.)"}]," Practitioners who have made evaluation the mechanism that steers development (82%\nagree) report the signal is untrustworthy on four axes — invalid, unstable across identical runs,\ngamed within a quarter, and ",["$","strong","strong-1",{"children":"prohibitively expensive"}],": the environment build is the work, \"what\nyou output is just a dataset and a score,\" and per-commit evaluation is priced out. 4a:["$","li","li-36",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"Replication does not certify a corpus, and treating it as if it does is this map's own blind\nspot."}]," A systematic fault in how evaluation items were ",["$","em","em-0",{"children":"constructed"}]," is inherited by every\nreplicate, so larger N, a converging mechanistic story, and even a controlled manipulation can all\nconfirm an effect that does not exist — documented end-to-end in one case where a shared decoding\nparameter fabricated a 32-point judge gap that survived N=50→N=500 and a producer-swap experiment,\nthen vanished to ceiling on correction. The structural remedy is to prefer ",["$","strong","strong-1",{"children":"oracle-bearing"}]," corpus\ndesigns: negatives produced by deterministic perturbation of a gold answer are checkable\nitem-by-item for free, while LLM-generated negatives are not checkable at all by any aggregate\nstatistic. 4e:["$","li","li-40",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":["When the misgrading proxy is what ",["$","em","em-0",{"children":"selects the agent's own improvements"}],", the error compounds\ninstead of merely misleading."]}]," Every proxy above misgrades an agent for a human reader. In a\nself-evolving system the proxy is in the control loop, and the ceiling becomes the ",["$","em","em-0",{"children":"judge's"}],", not the\nmodel's: an unlabeled test-time harness-evolution loop commits a harness at ",["$","strong","strong-1",{"children":"50.0%"}]," when a candidate\nin the pool it inspected scored ",["$","strong","strong-2",{"children":"64.0%"}]," (selection regret) and the best candidate ever generated\nscored ",["$","strong","strong-3",{"children":"70.0%"}]," (coverage gap). The judge is \"measurably miscalibrated\" — it prefers outputs that\n",["$","em","em-1",{"children":"execute cleanly but are wrong"}],", while claiming near-perfect correctness — and because proposer and\njudge share the frozen model, more search generates ",["$","strong","strong-4",{"children":"correlated variants repeating the same blind\nspots"}]," (more rounds can make it ",["$","em","em-2",{"children":"worse"}],"). The standing remedy — pair the LLM judge with a non-LLM\nground-truth gate — is precisely what the unlabeled setting removes. A system that rewrites itself\nagainst a proxy is bounded above by that proxy's false-positive profile. ",["$","strong","strong-5",{"children":"[T1, single-study]"}]]}]
4f:["$","li","li-41",{"children":["Corollary for practice: ",["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"do not select, compare, reward, or regress agents on a single outcome\nnumber."}]," Pair every headline metric with (a) the harness that produced it, (b) a trajectory audit\non a sample, (c) at least one proxy that fails in a different direction, (d) a ",["$","em","em-0",{"children":"distribution"}],"\nacross repeated runs, not a single attempt, and (e) — if the number is a ",["$","em","em-1",{"children":"comparison"}]," — a paired\nsame-model floor establishing that the gap exceeds the noise. 50:["$","li","li-42",{"children":[["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":["The misgrading now has a constructive dual: what you ",["$","em","em-0",{"children":"do"}]," grade is delivered to the point of\ndestroying what you don't."]}]," Every proxy failure catalogued above is a metric scoring bad work as\ngood. 59:["$","li","li-1",{"children":["2026-08-22 — two interaction edges, both about the grading layer rather than the model. ",["$","strong","strong-0",{"children":"(1)"}],"\nlego-rl-harness-native-training (Du, Jiang, Yuan et al., Huawei / CUHK, arXiv,\n",["$","a","a-0",{"href":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17393","target":"_blank","rel":"noreferrer","children":"\"LEGO-RL\""}],", 2026-08-18, ",["$","strong","strong-1",{"children":"T1"}],", strong on this edge) publishes what this\nmap has mostly had to reason about qualitatively: ",["$","strong","strong-2",{"children":"incidence rates for reward-integrity failures"}],", split\nby whether the agent exploited the reward or the environment produced one detached from the agent — git\nhistory 4.6–20.5%, test-file edits 2.4–19.4%, reference-fix download 1.9%, and ",["$","strong","strong-3",{"children":"2.5% of inspected tasks\nwith a verifier that applies the reference patch regardless"}],"\n(",["$","$L2","a-1",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/reward-hacking-in-agentic-training-has-measured-incidence-rates","children":"reward-hacking-in-agentic-training-has-measured-incidence-rates"}],"). Every other\nmember of this map's proxy catalogue is optimistic — the merge label, outcome pass rate, the LLM judge,\nself-report, resolution rate (",["$","$L2","a-2",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/resolution-rate-is-not-precision","children":"resolution-rate-is-not-precision"}],"), coverage on buggy code\n(",["$","$L2","a-3",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/coverage-stops-predicting-when-the-code-is-buggy","children":"coverage-stops-predicting-when-the-code-is-buggy"}],") — and an inverted proxy is worse, because it\nsurvives the sanity check by appearing to confirm exactly where damage is greatest. The general form,\nwhich this map should carry beyond the paper: ",["$","strong","strong-4",{"children":"an intervention's measured influence on a model is not a\ndirectional measure of its value"}]," — the same separation the skills work reached at outcome level\n(",["$","$L2","a-4",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/a-skill-description-acts-without-being-invoked","children":"a-skill-description-acts-without-being-invoked"}],", ",["$","$L2","a-5",{"href":"/agentic/concepts/skills-regress-as-much-as-they-gain","children":"skills-regress-as-much-as-they-gain"}],"), arrived\nat here through the hidden state.
